<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">                &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">PostMeta</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> date</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">post.date</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> minutes</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">post.readingTime</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> /></span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">          &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">          &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> className</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"flex justify-between gap-12"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">            &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">article</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> className</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"typeset typeset-reading min-w-0 flex-1 max-w-reading"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">              &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">                Put a rounded card inside another rounded card with a little padding between them, give both the same radius, and</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">                something feels off. Most people can</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">&#x26;apos;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">t say what. The inner corners look too sharp next to the outer ones, and the</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">                gap between them bulges at each corner.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">              &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">              &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">h2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> id</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"the-rule"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>The rule&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">h2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">              &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">                When two surfaces share an even inset, the outer radius should equal the inner radius plus the padding between them.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">                Add the border width too, if there is one. That keeps the gap the same thickness all the way around the curve.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">              &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">              &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">blockquote</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">                &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>Outer radius = inner radius + padding.&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">              &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">blockquote</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">              &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">h2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> id</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"why-it-looks-wrong"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>Why equal radii look wrong&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">h2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">              &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">                A corner radius describes a circle. If both corners use the same circle but one sits inset from the other, the two arcs</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">                don</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">&#x26;apos;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">t share a centre. At the straight edges the gap is your padding; at the corner it</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">&#x26;apos;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">s wider, by roughly</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">" "</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">}</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">                &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">code</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>0.41 × radius&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">code</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>. Your eye reads that as the inner shape shrinking away from its frame.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">              &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">              &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">ul</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">                &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">li</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>The effect is strongest on small, tight insets like segmented controls and chips.&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">li</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">                &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">li</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>It nearly disappears once the padding passes about 24px.&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">li</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">                &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">li</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>Dark mode makes it more visible, because shadows give way to hairline rings.&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">li</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">              &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">ul</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">              &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">h2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> id</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"in-tailwind"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>Writing it in Tailwind&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">h2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">              &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">                With a radius scale, pick the inner token first and walk up the scale by the padding. Here a &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">code</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>12px&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">code</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">> panel</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">                sits in a &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">code</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>4px&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">code</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">> frame, so the frame takes &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">code</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>16px&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">code</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">              &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">              &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> className</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"not-typeset"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">                &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">CodeBlock</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> title</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"nested-surface.tsx"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> language</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"tsx"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> html</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">nestedSurface</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> /></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">              &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">              &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">                The image in the middle is not part of the chain. Its padding is &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">code</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>16px&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">code</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>, which would leave it a radius of</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">                only 4px; at that distance it reads as its own surface, so it keeps its own token.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">              &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">              &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">h2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> id</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"when-to-stop"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>When to stop&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">h2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">              &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">                The rule is about shapes your eye compares directly. Past roughly 24px of padding, or with deliberately uneven insets,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">                the layers stop reading as one object and each should keep the radius it would have on its own.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">              &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
